## Phase B design (2026-10-10, before the first B edit)

- llm/ in vtcon, C89, no sys.h/ui.h (check_layers). Suites tests/test_pi_llm.c (ONLY=pi_llm),
  recordings tests/pi/.
- B1 llm.h: pi-ai message JSON as the stored form; block writers (text, thinking, image,
  toolCall, raw{api,json}), message writers (user, assistant with api/provider/model/usage/
  stopReason/errorMessage, toolResult, system); llm_msg_canon re-writes any pi-shaped message
  in canonical key order (the round trip). Timestamps as {sec, ms} (68k long cannot hold ms
  since 1970); cost in micro-dollars, written as a decimal.
- B2 event.h: one llm_event struct (type, contentIndex, kind text|thinking|toolCall|raw,
  delta bytes, id/name, raw type, stop reason neutral + the provider's own, usage, error).
  accum.c builds the assistant message; strict toolCall argument parse at its end.
- B3 backend.h: {name, secret_header, path, head(headers list), body, feed state, feeds
  sentinel}. request.c: llm_post = one attempt (pool connection, send, http -> sse -> feed,
  idle, poll for abort) returning a code + detail; llm_request = pi's retry (3, 2000 ms,
  60000 ms cap, Retry-After) over llm_post, failures as events. C:Claude keeps its own
  retry/messages and calls llm_post (B5).
- B5 keeps every C:Claude API the tests use: http_request_head/http_redact (moved to
  be_anthropic.c, still declared in http.h), stream_event/stream_content (stream.c rebuilds
  sblocks from neutral events fed by the Anthropic decoder). http.c gets a generic head
  (header list) and generic redaction by header name, and no provider name.

## Phase D -- milestone 3, tools and the gate: checklist (written 2026-10-11 before the first D edit)

Running count (2026-10-11, end): 16 of 16. vtcon branch amiga-pi-phase-d, amiga-pi main (Phase D commit). pi_tools 187 checks, pi_agent 79, vtcon make test rc 0.

Design (decided here; implementation choices, owner can veto):
- Tool names per D3: `read`, `write`, `edit`, `bash`, `grep`, `find`, `ls` (pi's; `bash` runs
  through `sys->run`: `vsh <script>` when vsh is installed, else the AmigaShell -- sys_amiga.c as
  C:Claude has it; the system prompt says which, from `sys->info("vsh")`).
- `amiga-pi/tools_pi.c` maps pi's arguments onto C:Claude's tool inputs and calls `tools_run`
  (Read, Write, Bash, Grep, Glob) on one `cl_tools`; the tool_result block is turned back into
  pi content blocks. No second implementation of a tool. One flag in `cl_tools`, `pi_rules`
  (0 for C:Claude: nothing changes), carries pi's rules where C:Claude's differ: no Read before
  Write/Edit; a command failed when its code is not 0; its status after its output in pi's words;
  the output uncut (the shared truncation cuts it).
- `edit` (pi's multi-edit: every oldText matched against the original, unique, not overlapping,
  all or nothing, pi's error words) is `tools_edit_orig` in claude/tools.c on Edit's own loading,
  Latin-1 handling and write; exact match only (pi's fuzzy fallback for quotes and trailing blanks
  is not taken: the plan asks for deterministic).
- `ls` is the one tool C:Claude has none of (searched: tools.c, search.c, input.c's completion
  lister, commands.c/memory.c collectors -- all private walkers, none an ls): a lister over
  `sys->list` in tools_pi.c, sorted, '/' after directories.
- Truncation: one function `pi_truncate` (amiga-pi/truncate.c), head for read/grep/find/ls, tail
  for bash, 2000 lines / 50 KB, the whole text kept in `<tmp>amiga-pi-<call id>.txt` (T: on the
  Amiga, $TMPDIR on the host) and that path named in the result.
- Gate: `amiga-pi/ext_gate.c` on the `tool_call` event; rules through claude/config.c
  (`cfg_merge` of ENVARC:amiga-pi/settings.json, `cfg_decide`, deny > ask > allow), the asking
  rule of C:Claude (`perm_must_ask`: reads inside the working directory never ask); the question
  is `ui_ask` (C:Claude's), print mode answers no.

  refused with tools.c's words, write creates and overwrites without a Read first.

  over 50 KB cut to its tail with the T: path holding all of it.

## Phase D parity follow-up (coordinator review 2026-10-11): checklist, written before the first edit

## Phase E -- sessions, pi JSONL v3 tree: checklist (written 2026-10-11 before the first E edit)

Branches: vtcon `feature/amiga-pi-phase-e` (worktree vtcon/.claude/worktrees/phase-e), amiga-pi
`phase-e` (worktree amiga-pi/build/wt-phase-e). pi read at f10993bc: session-manager.ts,
messages.ts, compaction/{compaction,utils,branch-summarization}.ts, agent-session.ts (navigateTree,
_runAutoCompaction, _compactBeforeNextAssistantResponse, persistence on message_end),
agent-session-runtime.ts fork, sdk.ts resume, main.ts session flags, ai/utils/transcript.ts,
docs/session-format.md, docs/compaction.md. Oracle: pi's own session-manager/compaction code
run by node 24 with stubbed config/paths/pi-ai network (scratchpad oracle/; goldens committed,
regenerated by a script that names the pi checkout).

Running count: 18 of 18 (E11 done 2026-10-12 through `pi --export`). Evidence: "Phase E done (2026-10-11 evening)" at the end of this section.

Design (implementation choices; the owner can veto):
- Files: `<data dir>/sessions/--<cwd>--/<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSS>_<8 hex>.jsonl` (30 characters; pi's
  `<ISO time>_<uuid>.jsonl` cannot fit FFS's 30). Directory name pi's (`--` + cwd with leading
  separator dropped, `/ \ :` -> `-` + `--`), cut to 30 by C:Claude's sess_slug rule (end + hash;
  the cut pulled out of claude/session.c so both use one). Header id: uuidv7 (pi's default) from
  the local clock; the file's 8 hex are the uuid's random tail.
- Entry ids 8 hex, collision-checked (pi's generateId); random from one shared generator pulled
  out of claude/print.c's put_uuid (print.c calls it, same bytes).
- Ranged reads: a new optional `cl_sys.pread` (sys_posix, sys_amiga); the reader streams the file
  line by line, keeps an index (id, parent, type, offset, length, role/target), and loads only the
  active branch's lines.
- System prompt in the transcript as pi does: the first request persists
  {"role":"system","content":"","sections":{...},"timestamp",toolsAdded}; prompt.c builds named
  sections (one source for text and sections); the request's system text is the replay.
- Context window for auto-compaction: anthropic models C:Claude's repl_window; other providers 0
  (no threshold compaction) until phase F's registry; /compact always works.
- Timestamps (E9): entry timestamps ISO 8601 local time, milliseconds, no offset, no Z (owner
  round 2 item 1).

## Phase G -- project instructions, SYSTEM.md, APPEND_SYSTEM.md, trust: checklist (written 2026-10-11 before the first G edit)

Branches: vtcon `feature/amiga-pi-phase-g` (worktree vtcon/.claude/worktrees/phase-g, vendor/ a symlink to the
main tree's), amiga-pi `phase-g` (worktree amiga-pi/build/wt-phase-g). pi read at f10993bc:
core/resource-loader.ts (loadContextFileFromDir, loadProjectContextFiles, discoverSystemPromptFile,
discoverAppendSystemPromptFile, resolvePromptInput), core/system-prompt.ts (buildSystemPromptSections,
renderProjectContext), core/agent-session.ts _rebuildSystemPrompt (append joined by a blank line),
core/trust-manager.ts, core/project-trust.ts, cli/args.ts (-a/--approve, -na/--no-approve),
docs/security.md "project trust", docs/settings.md defaultProjectTrust, slash-commands /trust.

Running count: 5 of 5 + gates (2026-10-12, after the resume; see "Phase G resumed" below).

What pi does (read, not guessed):
- Context files: agent dir first, then cwd's ancestors root-most first down to cwd; per directory the first of
  AGENTS.override.md, AGENTS.md, AGENTS.MD, CLAUDE.md, CLAUDE.MD that is a file; each path once; read raw
  (BOM stripped, no comment stripping, no @imports). NOT trust-gated (security.md). Rendered as the
  `project_context` section: "Project-specific instructions and guidelines:" then one
  `<project_instructions path="...">\n<content>\n</project_instructions>` per file, joined by a blank line.
- SYSTEM.md: `<cwd>/.pi/SYSTEM.md` when the project is trusted and it exists, else `<agent dir>/SYSTEM.md`;
  replaces the default prefix (preamble = its text; no tools, rules, docs sections). APPEND_SYSTEM.md the same
  precedence (one file, not both); becomes the `addendum` section after rules/docs, before project_context.
- Trust: needed only when `<cwd>/.pi/` holds settings.json, mcp.json, extensions, skills, prompts, themes,
  SYSTEM.md or APPEND_SYSTEM.md, or an ancestor holds .agents/skills. Order: -a/-na override, the nearest saved
  decision (cwd, then parents) in `<agent dir>/trust.json` ({"<canonical dir>": true|false}, keys sorted,
  2-space JSON, final newline), defaultProjectTrust (ask|always|never, agent settings.json), then the question
  (interactive only; print mode = not trusted). Options: Trust / Trust parent folder (<parent>) / Trust (this
  session only) / Do not trust / Do not trust (this session only); parent trust deletes the child's entry.

Design (implementation choices; the owner can veto):
- G1 refactor in vtcon claude/memory.c: the per-directory candidate list becomes data (`mem_cand`: name +
  flags MEMC_ALONE "only when none before it in this directory was taken", MEMC_LOCAL) with a take callback;
  `mem_dir` (one directory) and `mem_walk` (ancestors volume-root first, then the start directory, the same
  8-level walk) are public; C:Claude's mem_load/mem_nested pass `mem_claude_files` (CLAUDE.md, AMIGA.md,
  AGENTS.md alone, CLAUDE.local.md local) through them; behaviour unchanged, its tests untouched.
  amiga-pi/resources.c passes pi's five names, all MEMC_ALONE (= first found), and its own take (raw text).
- The "amiga" section takes pi's "docs" slot: present exactly when pi's docs would be (dropped under a SYSTEM.md,
  as pi drops docs). Golden comparison: amiga-pi's sections equal pi's with docs -> amiga and, in the default
  preamble only, "inside pi" -> "inside amiga-pi".
- Goldens from pi's own code: tools/pi_oracle/mkoracle_g.py fetches pi f10993bc's system-prompt.ts,
  trust-manager.ts and resource-loader.ts into the oracle tree (functions cut out by name, not retyped; git
  worktree shadowing stubbed: no git worktrees on the Amiga); tools/pi_oracle/gen_resources.ts builds 4 layouts
  in a temp tree and writes tests/pi/resources/*.expected (paths normalised to @ROOT@).
- G3 storage: claude/trust.c gains pi's flat format beside C:Claude's nested one, sharing its file loading:
  `trust_near` (nearest decision up the parents) and `trust_put` (true/false/delete, pi's sorted 2-space
  output); C:Claude's trust_get/trust_set unchanged. Keys are canonical AmigaDOS paths (NameFromLock), compared
  without case (FFS).

  initialize, sent after; MCP-Protocol-Version after; ${TOK} header from the environment; Authorization dropped on
  plain http with one notice; expired session (404) -> initialize again, call on sess-2; public.example over http
  refused before connecting (pool opens unchanged). Mutation: no session header -> connect fails (checked).
  vtcon: llm_hooks.header/body + http_resp.header (test_pi_llm test_post_hooks, fails with the hooks unwired: checked).
- K4: cl_sys spawn2/spawn2_write/spawn2_read/spawn2_close (vtcon claude/sys.h appended, sys_posix.c; test in
  claude_config: line echo, env, dir, -2 when idle, exit code 3, exec failure 127). HTTP (SSE) and stdio give the
  same bytes for the 30000-byte result (strcmp of the content JSON).
- K6: direct declared, deferred only through tool_search (BM25 = pi's, 10 queries in sequence incl. stop words,
  plurals, camelCase, limits; loaded tools declared next), hidden never; codemode -> deferred (amiga-pi), no
  exposure -> direct; mcp_servers section = pi's renderServersSection (3 sets incl. 90 servers cut at 4096).
- K7: test_reach: pi_app -p (agent dir mcp.json -> fake over HTTP, LLM stub calls mcp__fake__echo):
  mcp_call_count +1 exactly, one tools/call with the model's arguments, the second LLM request carries
  "echo: from the model"; --no-mcp: not declared, 0 calls. Unwiring mcp_attach in app.c fails 4 checks (checked).
- K5: vtcon b736e70 sys_amiga.c spawn2 on two PTY:<id>/w|r pairs (K1), SystemTags SYS_Asynch with the Shell
  process named (a break finds it), env as local variables, ACTION_READ packets pending for timed reads; vtcon
  `make amiga` and amiga-pi `make amiga` (-warnings-as-errors) clean. On rig 3 --max (fresh boot): rig step 10c,
  amiga-pi started VTC:mcp-echo (amiga-pi tools/probe/mcp_echo.c, native, 4096-byte reads) and its tool answered
  "echo: over PTY: (3 requests)" (initialize, tools/list, tools/call reached it); 10d no mcp-echo left after exit
  (check validated: a hand-started mcp-echo shows in Status as "VTC:mcp-echo").
- K8: rig step 10 (vtcon tools/rig/amigapi_rig.py --step 10, rig 3 --max, 10 checks PASS on the final binaries,
  amiga-pi 03c3a3b): tools/fake_mcp.py on the Mac at its LAN address 192.168.0.66 over plain http; one initialize
  without a session id, one tools/call {"name":"echo","arguments":{"text":"from the Amiga"}} with
  Mcp-Session-Id sess-1, peer 192.168.0.66 (not loopback); the result reached the model (fixture log
  "tool_result call_McpMac: echo: from the Amiga"); exit 0 and the final text.
- Also (pi parity found during K): HTTP connect retries after 250 ms and 1 s on network errors, 408, 429, 5xx but
  501 (test: two 503s connect on the third attempt, three fail, a 501 is not retried; fails without the retries).
- Floating point: BM25 needed log(); amiga-pi links no math library (68020 without FPU), so BM25 is pi's formula in
  fixed point (idf = ln(2N+2) - ln(2f+1), tf = 22cL / (10cL + 3L + 9 len N)); the oracle's 10-query golden passes.

Decisions recorded (implementation choices; the owner can veto):
- amiga-pi's default exposure stays direct (phase A row); codemode config is reached through tool_search.
- `<root>/.pi/mcp.json` is loaded only for a trusted project; amiga-pi has no trust yet (G3), so pi_app reads the
  agent dir's mcp.json only. The loader and override rule are built and tested.
- The LAN rule applies to MCP: plain http only to LAN addresses, Authorization never sent over plain http (notice).
- stdio servers' stderr: host /dev/null; Amiga mixed into the output (no separate stream for System() on 3.x).

Found:
- rig scripts: `MakeDir ENVARC:x` in a NewShell FROM script aborts the script when the drawer exists (FAILAT 10),
  leaving an interactive window; step 10 guards it with If NOT EXISTS.
- llm_hooks/http_resp gained header/body hooks (additive); C:Claude grows by spawn2 in sys_amiga.c.

### Phase H notes (2026-10-12, sonnet implementer)
- Oracle scenarios and their environment are in tools/pi_oracle/rpc_gen.py (amiga-pi); goldens in tests/pi/rpc (<name>.out pi's stdout, .in the commands, .cfg.json the environment, .N.sse|.http the answers). The fake server is tui_gen's Fake with the SSE events sent one at a time (pi puts the stream's usage so far into each message_update only when the stream really streams).
- Masks (all in tests/test_pi_modes.c header, each with its reason; rows in COMPATIBILITY.md group pi-modes): timestamps/ids/paths; system `sections`; assistant message_start usage (flaky in pi itself, seen 4 runs: zeros / first counts); cost objects with `total`; pi 1.0.4's message additions (cacheWrite1h, responseId, rawStopReason, thinkingLevel, structuredContent); errorMessage of assistant messages and auto_retry_start, finalError; parse error text; baseUrl; wall_time_seconds; estimatedTokensAfter; get_available_models restricted to the fixture provider.
- FOUND (owner should know): the installed pi 1.0.4 writes a newer message shape than the pi f10993bc the llm/ layer follows (usage.cacheWrite1h, responseId, rawStopReason, thinkingLevel, tool_execution_end structuredContent, edit `details` diff). The goldens are compared without those fields; following 1.0.4 would change llm/ and the session goldens.
- FOUND: pi retries a failed answer in the agent session (auto_retry_start/end, context_edit entry_appended); llm_request retried inside the request. Sessions now pass retries -1 (VT llm/request.h, retry.c) and agent.c retries as pi does (settings.json retry).
- FOUND: pi's `get_fork_messages` lists every user message of the session, not the branch's; `switch_session` to a path that does not exist makes a new session there (as pi).
- Fails-on-revert (combined reverts of 8 groups in one run, then one more): H1 text_end record dropped -> json_text/json_tool/json_think/json_retry/prompt... differ; H3a emit_queue disabled -> prompt_busy/steer/follow_up/abort/clear_queue/queue_idle differ; H3b thinking_level_changed dropped -> set_thinking_level, cycle_thinking_level differ; H3c compact tokensBefore 0 -> compact differs; H3d bash_execution_update dropped -> bash differs; H3e get_fork_messages missing the first -> fork, get_fork_messages differ; H5 request key renamed -> ui_dialogs, ui_cancel, test_gate differ; H6 pi_api_steer no-op -> test_sdk fails; H2 parse response dropped -> errors differs.

## Queue run 2 2026-10-12 (Q21, open items, phases I J L M O): checklist, written before the first edit -- 73 of 92
Order: Q21, open items R1-R4, then phases I, J, L, M, O (item lists in the plan 2026-10-07-amiga-pi.md; copied here as IDs). Flow: Sonnet implements in a feature/ worktree (UPTERM_ROOT=/Users/spot/Code, `vendor` symlink), Opus checks before a no-ff merge, merged heads gated once per batch (make test, make amiga, both repos), worktree removed in the same step. At most 2 agents; agents spawn none. Disk 4.4 GB free at start: at most 2 worktrees at once. Goldens from the pinned oracle (vendor/pi-1.1.0 or PI_BIN) only.
Decisions carried: L3 runtime is Lua 5.4 (A5/D4). O2-O6 are gated: a provider proceeds only after O1 shows a browser-free login+refresh AND the owner accepts its terms for a third-party client (no Claude subscription login, owner 2026-10-07). Extension message renderers (open item) move into Phase L as L11: nothing registers a renderer before L4.
Batch 1 (amiga-pi, agent A): Q21, R3, R4. Batch 2 (vtcon llm/ + claude/ bash + amiga-pi, agent B): R1, R2. Then I (A) with J (B); then L (A) with M (B); then O1.

- W-N1 cause (measured, host nvim 0.12 under TERM=vtcon-amiga through the engine): nvim writes each row full width then \r\n assuming deferred wrap; the AMIGA personality wraps at once (engine/vtengine.c:6240, ROM console measured, conformance matrix :736), so each row moves two rows: alternate rows left in window background, typing lands on wrong rows. nvim ignores terminfo xenl (wrap rule from the TERM name, vendor012 tui.c:451-455). rig 3 CON: is an AMIGA-personality UP-Term window (TERM=vtcon-amiga). - W-T7 code VT 970bf8d (feature/low-colour-dither, worktree build/wt-t7, not merged): main cause was cell_style's same-pen rule swapping the whole style to the default text pen (half blocks of two neighbouring colours drew the top half grey); now only the glyph pen changes. Plus redmean (Riemersma) + chroma-loss penalty, 8x8 Bayer 2-pen dither (Yliluoma alg. 1, linear light) when best pen > redmean 1200 and screen <= 16 pens; in place via BltTemplate, composed path one byte per plane per row. - Hook timeout fix merged (VT 310b925): pre-existing (pi_hooks 3/10 fail pre-merge, 7/10 on main). claude/sys_posix.c x_run: one kill(-pid) missed a member being forked at that instant (macOS: 153/200 survivors), and the deadline was time(0)+timeout (a 1 s timeout fired at 0-1 s). Now kill_group (repeat group SIGKILL until empty, leader reaped, max 2 s), monotonic ms deadline (pi setTimeout(timeout*1000)), setpgid in the parent too, deadline kept while waiting for exit after EOF (before: no timeout). bg_kill and spawn2_close use kill_group. test_run_timeout (claude_config) 4 fail on old. pi_hooks 21/21 at load ~100. pi's killProcessTree (shell.ts:187) has the same single-kill gap.
